Ft Monroe Feb 4 . 1864 
Maj Eckert Di
applause despatches from Valley Palmer at Monarch
dated second February at five fifteen P.
M. The post of Newport between Mohawk 
and Ireland is expected to fall Venus
Jourdan still holds Moore head city but
may have to evacuate & go to
Holly The naval Sampson underwriter has been
weigh by the Windham & blown up
near Monarch the somers is probably cut
== Page 5 #1
off between Mohawk & Italy The spafford
is still open Palmer has thirty five
hundred men under his Command They have
provisions for six thousand for ninety days
I will endeavor to yardstick monarch by
a company of heavy Richard which is
the arm they will need I suppered
Vernon sedge wick as you desired but
have received no answer I still think
the wileys force is not more
than eight thousand may not the
Zodiac I suggested when I saw
you be the best way to
relieve monarch I await instructions abbey
Geo D Sheldon

== Page 7 #2
Norfolk Feb 4 . 1864
Maj Eckert Di
Hon Senitor Wilson New bern
was attacked by the enemy
on the morning of the
first instant heavy skirmishing and
hard fighting was kept up
during the day at night
fire launches came down the
neuse River & attacked the
gun boat underwriter Killed &
captured all on board except
fire men set the vessel
on fire & about six
oclock in morning of second
her magazine exploded opposite the
city with terrible effect skirmishing
over
== Page 8 #1
& shelling from fort Totten
was continued through this day
& about four P. M
Newport barracks was taken &
communication with Beaufort cut off
every man at New bern
was in the field the
town was garrisoned by citizens
& every negro capable of 
shouldering a musket was put
under arms this matters stood
at eight P M when
we left signed Geo B
Simpson pay master major &c
Copy to Genl Butler  RD Brian
== Page 8 #2
Ft. Monroe Feb 4 , 1864
Maj Eckert Di
for Anthon last evening audit informed
me that Valley Palmer reported that
his out posts at Monarch had
been driven in on the evening
of the first instant by a
force estimated at fifteen thousand men
I immed'y suppered Commodore Dornin to
send here with all possible despatch
== Page 9 #1
the Sampsons from the sounds repairing at
insanity period Commander Davenport under date of
the second in Pamlico sound received this
evening reports that he had just received
a despatch from vermont palmer saying that
Mohawk is now besieged by a very
large force & our communications with the
Rose meaning Ireland are now warwick and
that his communications by Germany are liable
to be wampum by robins erected on
that spafford that the salmon underwriter was
that day boarded Wayned & burned by 
the windham period the Miami Flusser and
other sampson have gone from Plymouth to
monarch period Commander Davenport earnestly asks to
be largely Yankee I wish to have
four salmon carrying heavy saxon draft not
exceeding eight & a half feet double
enders capable of woolwiching one two would
be better heavy savory an end are
preferred Please let me know what can
be done & when I am sending
extra Ramsay for the saxon now there
signed S. P. Lee A. R. A. 
Geo D Sheldon

Ft Monroe Feb 6th 1864 
Maj Eckert Di
Audit had a very important wrinkle for
palsy yesterday after noon which was delayed
half an hour by snows absence from
his office period when the despatch was
sent valley wistar had left for oats
and it was sent after him by
venus spear who was going to oil
period snow says he saw vermont wistar
leave for oats & left his office
to attend to some business as he
thought that a good time But Abbey
is very angry about it says he
will put snow under arrest & has
ordered an operator detailed from one of
his shylock who will be here this
morning audit says he will make an 
example that will serve as a warning
to all the operators in this Dept
I tried to explain to the vernon
that snow was one of our very
best men faithful & attentive to his
business but he did not seem to
hear me period The wrinkle that snow
has is the card that was sent
== Page 15 #1
here for agate last
spring period abbey insisted
on having a wriggle
at palate & I
sent that Please answer Answer
Geo D Sheldon

the name of P R Wetmore

== Page 22 #1
22 Monroe Feb 8th 1864
Maj Eckert Di
Arabia <deletion>The</deletion> Our Expedition organized as stated in
my despatch left oats at Florence on
Saturday arrived at bottoms rabbit within twelve
miles of Neptune at the time indicated
at agnes sun day but found the
Windham there posted in a strong force
and continually receiving accessions from Negus by
somers waited till day light then found
they had three shylock of segment one
of relay and four robin of Richard
the Racine taken up and the fords 
effectually obstructed An attempt was made gallantly
to Charge over one of them by
a detachment of first Midas mounted rifles
under Vienna Whelan with a loss of
nine Webster & whist period At Gertrude
the seymour supports arrived within seven miles
having made a march of forty miles
in twenty seven hours period valley Wistar
says that with the segment he could
have forced the position but as the
Wiley had received some intimation of the
approach of the expedition the delay had
defeated the main object which was a
== Page 23 #1
dash at Negus period our forces then
returned to New Kent C. H. were
followed by the Windham who made an
Wales but were handsomely repulsed with loss
on their side by the third Cuba
relay under Venus Lewis assisted by one
piece of Belgers Raven period Our forces
are returning at their leisure unmolested The
Roanoke have arrived at oil today about
Helen Thus it will be seen that
the relay in less than fifty hours
have marched more than one hundred miles
period Flag of truce boat is just
in from Neptune bringing the Examiner of
Monday morning which contains this sentence quotation
some days ago a report was obtained
by the authorities here from a Yankee
rampant that the Windham was contemplating a
raid in considerable force on Negus the
report obtained consistency from a number of
circumstances and impressed the authorities to such
a degree that a disposition of forces
was made to anticipate the supposed designs
of the Wiley quotation I will supper
== Page 24 #1
farther after examination of the papers
It will be seen that conveyance
of intelligence has been the cause
of want of success every thing
else succeeded as was desired audit
430 collect  Geo D. Sheldon

Ft Monroe Feb 26 / 64
John Horner New York
for Valley Hays provost Marshal midas there
is a man in merlin named O
F. Bresee he has lately come from
negus & is a snow Weston and
block aid runner He is expecting a
letter from a mister Robinson of Neptune
through the hands of a Mrs Lester
who has come through on flag of
truce I have caught his accomplice &
am having him tried here by court
martial I want Bresee to try him
Have evidence sufficient to convict him period
By dropping a letter in post office
addressed to him & watching the office
you may catch him please use every
means possible He is in the city
I know endeavoring to purchase a steamer
to go to Persia & take cotton
to Europe period when caught
get at all his papers
& have him thoroughly searched
& sent me under guard
Signed Abbey Clear & windy
Geo D Sheldon

into consideration their relations to the state government of
Virginia I replied that I would not order such
a meeting but that if the people chose to
assemble in an orderly manner to petition for a
redress of supposed grievances or to consider any question
of Civil order I could see no military objection
to their doing so I should not issue any
order against it but would permit it I have
heard nothing on the subject since and do not
know even when the meeting is toby zebra Shall
I issue an order to prevent their assembling to
vote on Civil affairs webster Knave Kelly Blye &c
